in Euclidean dS, namely the
d
-dimensional sphere
S^{d}
. following the treatment of
[
87
]
. Then, we make an analytic continuation from EdS to dS to obtain the spectral function
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
in dS. Then, we present the pole structure of the spectral function on the complex
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}
plane, which is useful for our evaluation of the spectral integral. We then discuss the divergence of the spectral function in 3 spatial dimensions. Finally, we derive some useful properties of the
\Pi
function defined in (
45
).

B.1
Derivation of the spectral function

The derivations in this subsection closely follow
[
87
]
.

Bubble function on a sphere.

On a
(d+1)
-dimensional sphere
S^{d+1}
, the 2-point function
D(x,y)
of the massive scalar field can be decomposed in terms of spherical harmonics
Y_{\vec{L}}(x)
. Here
\vec{L}=(L,L_{d},L_{d-1},\cdots,L_{1})
are a vector of
d+1
integer entries satisfying
L\geq L_{d}\geq\cdots\geq L_{2}\geq|L_{1}|
.

\displaystyle D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(x,y)=\sum_{\vec{L}}\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{\lambda_{L\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}}Y_{\vec{L}}(x)Y_{\vec{L}}^{*}(y)=\sum_{L=0}^{\infty}\displaystyle\frac{\,{\Gamma(d/2)(L+d/2)}\,}{2\pi^{d/2+1}\lambda_{L\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}}\mathrm{C}_{L}^{d/2}(Z),

(110)

where
\lambda_{L\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}=(L+d/2)^{2}+\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}
,
Z
is the imbedding distance between
x
and
y
,
\mathrm{C}_{m}^{\alpha}(z)
is the Gegenbauer polynomial, and we have used the following summation formula:

\displaystyle\sum_{L_{1},\cdots,L_{d}}Y_{\vec{L}}(x)Y_{\vec{L}}^{*}(y)=\displaystyle\frac{\,{\Gamma(d/2)(2L+d)}\,}{4\pi^{d/2+1}}\mathrm{C}_{L}^{d/2}(Z_{xy}),

(111)

On the other hand, the products of two propagators
D^{2}(x,y)
is again a rotational invariant 2-point function. Suppose we can decompose it in the following way

\displaystyle D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{2}(x,y)=\sum_{\vec{L}}\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{EdS}}(L)Y_{\vec{L}}(x)Y_{\vec{L}}^{*}(y)=

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{\,{\Gamma(d/2)}\,}{2\pi^{d/2+1}}\sum_{L=0}^{\infty}(L+d/2)B_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{EdS}}(L)\mathrm{C}_{L}^{d/2}(Z_{xy})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{\,{\Gamma(d/2)}\,}{2\pi^{d/2+1}}\sum_{L=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{L}(L+d/2)B_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{EdS}}(L)\mathrm{C}_{L}^{d/2}(-Z_{xy}),

(112)

where
B_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{EdS}}
is the bubble function in the
L
space. Using the orthogonality of the Gegenbauer polynomials:

\displaystyle\int_{-1}^{1}{\mathrm{d}}x\,(1-x^{2})^{\alpha-1/2}\mathrm{C}_{m}^{\alpha}(x)\mathrm{C}_{n}^{\alpha}(x)=\displaystyle\frac{\,{2^{1-2\alpha}\pi}\,}{n+\alpha}\Gamma\begin{bmatrix}n+2\alpha\\
n+1,\alpha,\alpha\end{bmatrix}\delta_{mn},

(113)

we can find

\displaystyle B_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{EdS}}(L)=

\displaystyle~(4\pi)^{d/2}\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}L+1,d/2\\
L+d\end{matrix}\right]\int_{-1}^{1}{\mathrm{d}}Z\,(1-Z^{2})^{(d-1)/2}\mathrm{C}_{L}^{d/2}(Z)D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{2}(Z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~(4\pi)^{d/2}\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}L+1,d/2\\
L+d\end{matrix}\right]\int_{-1}^{1}{\mathrm{d}}Z\,(1-Z^{2})^{(d-1)/2}

\displaystyle~\times\sum_{M,N=0}^{\infty}\displaystyle\frac{\,{\Gamma^{2}(d/2)(M+d/2)(N+d/2)}\,}{4\pi^{d+2}\lambda_{M\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\lambda_{N\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}}\mathrm{C}_{L}^{d/2}(Z)\mathrm{C}_{M}^{d/2}(Z)\mathrm{C}_{N}^{d/2}(Z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{4^{d/2-1}}\,}{\pi^{d/2+2}}\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}L+1,\{d/2\}^{(3)}\\
L+d\end{matrix}\right]\sum_{M,N=0}^{\infty}\displaystyle\frac{\,{(M+d/2)(N+d/2)}\,}{\lambda_{M\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\lambda_{N\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}}

\displaystyle~\times\int_{-1}^{1}{\mathrm{d}}Z\,(1-Z^{2})^{(d-1)/2}\mathrm{C}_{L}^{d/2}(Z)\mathrm{C}_{M}^{d/2}(Z)\mathrm{C}_{N}^{d/2}(Z).

(114)

Here we need to quote a result for the integral of three Gegenbauer polynomials of the same degree:

\displaystyle\int_{-1}^{1}{\mathrm{d}}Z\,(1-Z^{2})^{\alpha-1/2}\mathrm{C}_{L}^{\alpha}(Z)\mathrm{C}_{M}^{\alpha}(Z)\mathrm{C}_{N}^{\alpha}(Z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}2^{1-2\alpha}\pi\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}K+2\alpha,K-L+\alpha,K-M+\alpha,K-N+\alpha\\
\{\alpha\}^{(4)},K+\alpha+1,K-L+1,K-M+1,K-N+1\end{matrix}\right];\\
\text{(when $L,M,N\in\mathbb{N}_{0}$ satisfy the triangle inequalities and $K\equiv\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}(L+M+N)\in\mathbb{N}_{0}$)}\\
0.~~~\text{(otherwise)}\end{array}\right.

where
K\equiv(L+M+N)/2
.
Using this formula, we get

\displaystyle B_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{EdS}}(L)=

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{8\pi^{d/2+1}}\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}L+1\\
L+d,\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}\end{matrix}\right]\sum_{(M,N)}\Lambda_{M\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Lambda_{N\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}

\displaystyle~\times\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}K+d,K-L+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},K-M+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},K-N+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}\\
K+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+1,K-L+1,K-M+1,K-N+1\end{matrix}\right],

(118)

where
\Lambda_{M\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\equiv(2M+d)/\lambda_{M\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
, and the notation
(M,N)
means that the summation goes over all values satisfying the triangle inequality.
The next step is to remove the constraint on the variables
(M,N)
. Following the method in
[
87
]
, we define new variables:

\displaystyle I=K-L,

\displaystyle J=K-N.

(119)

Then

\displaystyle M=I+J,

\displaystyle N=I-J+L,

\displaystyle K=I+L.

(120)

Then the summation becomes

\displaystyle\sum_{I=0}^{\infty}\sum_{J=0}^{L}\Lambda_{I+J,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Lambda_{I-J+L,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Gamma\begin{bmatrix}I+L+d,I+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},L-J+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},J+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}\\
I+L+1+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},I+1,L-J+1,J+1\end{bmatrix}

(121)

First, consider the
J
sum:

\displaystyle\sum_{J=0}^{L}\Lambda_{I+J,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Lambda_{I-J+L,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Gamma\begin{bmatrix}L-J+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},J+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}\\
L-J+1,J+1\end{bmatrix}.

(122)

Due to the factor
\Gamma[L-J+1,J+1]
in the denominator, the summand automatically vanishes when
J
takes integer values in the range
J<0
and
J>L
. Therefore, we can expand the summation range to entire real integers:

\displaystyle\sum_{J=-\infty}^{\infty}\Lambda_{I+J,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Lambda_{I-J+L,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Gamma\begin{bmatrix}L-J+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},J+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}\\
L-J+1,J+1\end{bmatrix}.

(123)

Then we can rewrite this summation as an integral:

\displaystyle\oint\displaystyle\frac{\,{{\mathrm{d}}J}\,}{2\pi\mathrm{i}}\pi\cot(\pi J)\Lambda_{I+J,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Lambda_{I-J+L,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Gamma\begin{bmatrix}L-J+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},J+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}\\
L-J+1,J+1\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\oint\displaystyle\frac{\,{{\mathrm{d}}J}\,}{2\pi\mathrm{i}}(-\cos\pi J)\Lambda_{I+J,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Lambda_{I-J+L,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Gamma\begin{bmatrix}L-J+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},J+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},-J\\
L-J+1\end{bmatrix}.

(124)

At large
J
, the integrand goes as
|J|^{d-4}
. So if we choose the contour to be a circle around the complex infinity, the whole integral is zero. Now, let us evaluate the same integral with the residue theorem. The integrand has the following set of poles:

1.

J=0,\cdots,L
, coming from
\Gamma(-J)/\Gamma(L-J+1)
, which simply gives the original sum.

2.

J=d/2+L+n
,
n=0,1,2,\cdots
, coming from
\Gamma(L-J+d/2)
.

3.

J=-d/2-n
,
n=0,1,2,\cdots
, coming from
\Gamma(J+d/2)
.

4.

J=-I-d/2\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
, coming from
\Lambda_{I+J,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
.

5.

J=I+L+d/2\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
, coming from
\Lambda_{I-J+L,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
.

The second set and the third set of poles give identical results. Each of the two contributions reads:

\displaystyle-\cos(\pi d/2)\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Lambda_{I+L+d/2+n,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Lambda_{I-d/2-n,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Gamma\begin{bmatrix}n+L+d,n+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}\\
n+1,n+L+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+1\end{bmatrix}.

(125)

The fourth and fifth sets of poles again give identical contributions. Each of the two sets gives:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{\,{\pi\cos[\pi(d/2-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)]}\,}{\sin(-\mathrm{i}\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\Lambda_{2I+L+d/2-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Gamma\begin{bmatrix}L+I+d-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,I+d/2-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
L+I+d/2-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1,I+1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{bmatrix}+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu).

(126)

Therefore, we get:

\displaystyle\sum_{J=0}^{L}\Lambda_{I+J,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Lambda_{I-J+L,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Gamma\begin{bmatrix}L-J+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},J+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}\\
L-J+1,J+1\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\bigg\{\displaystyle\frac{\,{2\pi\cos[\pi(d/2-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)]}\,}{\sin(\mathrm{i}\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\Lambda_{2I+L+d/2-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Gamma\begin{bmatrix}L+I+d-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,I+d/2-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
L+I+d/2-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1,I+1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{bmatrix}+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\bigg\}

\displaystyle~+2\cos(\pi d/2)\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Lambda_{I+L+d/2+n,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Lambda_{I-d/2-n,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Gamma\begin{bmatrix}n+L+d,n+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}\\
n+1,n+L+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+1\end{bmatrix}.

(127)

Next, consider the
I
sum. There are two types of contributions from the
J
sum. First, there is a
n
sum which contributes the following terms:

\displaystyle\sum_{I=0}^{\infty}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Lambda_{I+L+d/2+n,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Lambda_{I-d/2-n,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Gamma\begin{bmatrix}I+L+d,I+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},n+L+d,n+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}\\
I+1,I+L+1+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},n+1,n+L+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+1\end{bmatrix}.

(128)

As observed in
[
87
]
, the
\Gamma
products and
\Lambda_{I+L+d/2+n,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
is invariant under the change of variables
I\leftrightarrow n
, but
\Lambda_{I-d/2-n,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
changes sign. So this part of the
I
sum vanishes. Next, consider the rest contributions from the
J
sum:

\displaystyle\sum_{I=0}^{\infty}\Lambda_{2I+L+d/2-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Gamma\begin{bmatrix}I+L+d,I+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},L+I+d-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,I+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
I+1,I+L+1+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},L+I+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1,I+1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{I=0}^{\infty}\Gamma\begin{bmatrix}I+\mbox{$\frac{\,{L+d-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}+1,I+\mbox{$\frac{\,{L+d}\,}{2}$},I+\mbox{$\frac{\,{L+d}\,}{2}$}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
I+\mbox{$\frac{\,{L+d-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},I+\mbox{$\frac{\,{L+d}\,}{2}$}+1,I+\mbox{$\frac{\,{L+d}\,}{2}$}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\begin{bmatrix}I+L+d,I+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},L+I+d-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,I+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
I+1,I+L+1+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},L+I+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1,I+1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~{}_{7}\mathcal{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mbox{$\frac{\,{L+d-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}+1,\mbox{$\frac{\,{L+d}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{L+d}\,}{2}$}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,L+d,\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},L+d-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
\mbox{$\frac{\,{L+d-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{L+d}\,}{2}$}+1,\mbox{$\frac{\,{L+d}\,}{2}$}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1,L+1+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},L+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1,1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\middle|1\right].

(129)

At this point, we make use of a connection formula between two generalized hypergeometric functions
[
93
]
:

{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{A}\\
\mathbf{B}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]=\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{C}\\
\mathbf{D}\end{matrix}\right]{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{E}\\
\mathbf{F}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right],

(130)

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}=

\displaystyle~\{a,\mbox{$\frac{\,{a}\,}{2}$}+1,b,c,e,f,g\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{B}=

\displaystyle~\{\mbox{$\frac{\,{a}\,}{2}$},1+a-b,1+a-c,1+a-e,1+a-f,1+a-g\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{C}=

\displaystyle~\{1+a-f,1+a-g,2+2a-b-c-e,2+2a-b-c-e-f-g\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}=

\displaystyle~\{1+a,1+a-f-g,2+2a-b-c-e-f,2+2a-b-c-e-g\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}=

\displaystyle~\{1+2a-b-c-e,\tfrac{3}{2}+a-\tfrac{b}{2}-\tfrac{c}{2}-\tfrac{e}{2},

\displaystyle~~\,1+a-c-e,1+a-b-e,1+a-b-c,f,g\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{F}=

\displaystyle~\{\tfrac{1}{2}+a-\tfrac{b}{2}-\tfrac{c}{2}-\tfrac{e}{2},1+a-b,1+a-c,1+a-e,

\displaystyle~~\,2+2a-b-c-e-f,2+2a-b-c-e-g\}.

(131)

Now, we can make the following assignment of parameters:

\displaystyle a=L+d-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,

\displaystyle b=\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,

\displaystyle c=L+d,e=\frac{d}{2},

\displaystyle f=\frac{L+d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,

\displaystyle g=\frac{L+d}{2}.

(132)

Then, we can rewrite the hypergeometric function in (
B.1
) as:

\displaystyle\sum_{I=0}^{\infty}\Lambda_{2I+L+d/2-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Gamma\begin{bmatrix}I+L+d,I+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},L+I+d-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,I+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
I+1,I+L+1+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$},L+I+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1,I+1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}\tfrac{d}{2},2-d,\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,L+d,2+L-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{L+d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{L+d}{2}\\
1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,L+\tfrac{d}{2}+1,L+1+\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,2+\tfrac{L-d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,2+\tfrac{L-d}{2}\end{matrix}\right]

\displaystyle\times{}_{7}F_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}1+L-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{3+L-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},1-\tfrac{d}{2},1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-\tfrac{d}{2},1+L,\tfrac{L+d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{L+d}{2}\\
\tfrac{1+L-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},L+1+\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1+L+\tfrac{d}{2},1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,2+\tfrac{L-d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,2+\tfrac{L-d}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right].

(133)

It seems that we are making the expression more complicated. The reason for using the connection formula (
130
) is the following. We anticipate that the spectral function is divergent when
d=3
, which is nothing but the familiar UV divergence of the 1-loop bubble diagram. In (
B.1
), this divergence is somewhat hidden in the complicated parameter dependence in the hypergeometric function, making it difficult to isolate and subtract the UV divergence. After using the connection formula (
130
), we see that the divergence is manifested through the
\Gamma(2-d)
factor, which is particularly easy to deal with.

Now, combining (
B.1
) and (
B.1
), we get the final expression for the bubble function
B_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\left(L\right)
as:

\displaystyle B_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{EdS}}\left(L\right)=\frac{1}{8\pi^{d/2}}\frac{\cos\left[\pi\left(d/2-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\right)\right]}{\sin\left(i\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\right)}\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}2-d,\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,L+1,2+L-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{L+d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{L+d}{2}\\
1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,L+\tfrac{d}{2}+1,L+1+\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,2+\tfrac{L-d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,2+\tfrac{L-d}{2}\end{matrix}\right]

\displaystyle\times{}_{7}F_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}1+L-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{3+L-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},1-\tfrac{d}{2},1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-\tfrac{d}{2},1+L,\tfrac{L+d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{L+d}{2}\\
\tfrac{1+L-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},L+1+\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1+L+\tfrac{d}{2},1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,2+\tfrac{L-d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,2+\tfrac{L-d}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]+\left(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\right).

(134)

From the sphere to de Sitter.

Next, we derive the spectral function
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
in dS from the bubble function in EdS as obtained above. Following
[
87
]
, we use a Watson-Sommerfeld transformation to recast the summation in (
B.1
) into an integral:

\Big[D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(x,y)\Big]^{2}=\int_{\gamma}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}L}{2\pi\mathrm{i}}\frac{\pi}{\sin(\pi L)}\frac{\Gamma(d/2)}{2\pi^{d/2+1}}(L+d/2)B_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{EdS}}(L)\mathrm{C}_{L}^{d/2}(-Z_{xy}),

(135)

where the contour
\gamma
surrounds all the poles of
1/\sin\left(\pi L\right)
at non-negative integer values of
L
. At large
|L|\gg 1
the integrand decays like
e^{-\pi|\text{Im}L|}
, so the contour
\gamma
can be deformed to run along the imaginary axis with
\text{Re}\left[L\right]=-d/2
. Now, using the following formula for the Gegenbauer polynomial,

\mathrm{C}_{L}^{d/2}(-Z_{xy})=\displaystyle\frac{\,{(d)_{L}}\,}{L!}{}_{2}\mathrm{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}-L,d+L\\
\tfrac{d+1}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|\frac{1+Z_{xy}}{2}\right],

(136)

we find the following relation:

\frac{(L+d/2)\Gamma(d/2)\mathrm{C}_{L}^{d/2}(-Z_{xy})}{2\pi^{d/2}\sin(\pi L)}\bigg|_{L=-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}=-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}(x,y).

(137)

So equation (
135
) can be written as

\displaystyle\Big[D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(x,y)\Big]^{2}=

\displaystyle-\int_{-\infty+\mathrm{i}(-d/2+\epsilon)}^{\infty+\mathrm{i}(-d/2+\epsilon)}{\mathrm{d}}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\displaystyle\frac{\,{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}\,}{\pi\mathrm{i}}B_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{EdS}}\Big(-\displaystyle\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\Big)D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}(x,y).

(138)

Now, the propagator in EdS
D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(x,y)
can be analytically continued to the SK propagators
D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mathsf{ab}}(x,y)
in dS:

\displaystyle\Big[D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}}(x,y)\Big]^{2}=

\displaystyle-\int_{-\infty-\mathrm{i}\epsilon}^{\infty-\mathrm{i}\epsilon}{\mathrm{d}}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\displaystyle\frac{\,{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}\,}{\pi\mathrm{i}}B_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{EdS}}\Big(-\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\Big)D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mathsf{ab}}(x,y)+\text{poles from }\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\in(0,\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}).

(139)

Here we are moving the integration contour from
\text{Im}\,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=-d/2+\epsilon
to
\text{Im}\,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=-\epsilon
. Therefore, we need to include any poles in the region
\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\in(0,\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$})
, as indicated in the last term of (
139
). Below we shall show that there is actually no pole from this region for the parameters of our interest.

Therefore, the spectral density function
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\left(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\right)
in
\mathrm{dS}^{d}
is the opposite of which in the
d
-dimensional sphere
S^{d}
,
-B_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{EdS}}\left(-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\right)
.

\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})=-B^{\text{EdS}}\left(-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\right).

(140)

More explicitly,

\displaystyle\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{8\pi^{d/2}}\frac{\cos[\pi(-\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)]}{\sin(-\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}2-d,\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},2-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2}{2},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+d/2}{2}\\
1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-3d/2}{2},\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-3d/2}{2}\end{matrix}\right]

\displaystyle\times{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\left.\begin{matrix}1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{3-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},1-\tfrac{d}{2},1-\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2}{2},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+d/2}{2}\\
\tfrac{1-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-3d/2}{2},\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-3d/2}{2}\end{matrix}\right|1\right]

\displaystyle+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu).

(141)

To analyze the analytical property of this spectral function, it is useful to rewrite it in terms of the regularized hypergeometric function
{}_{7}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\mathrm{F}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{6}
as defined in (
105
):

\displaystyle\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{8\pi^{d/2}}\frac{\cos[\pi(-\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)]}{\sin(-\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}2-d,\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},2-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2}{2},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+d/2}{2},\tfrac{1-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2}\end{matrix}\right]

\displaystyle\times{}_{7}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\mathrm{F}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{6}\left[\left.\begin{matrix}1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{3-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},1-\tfrac{d}{2},1-\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2}{2},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+d/2}{2}\\
\tfrac{1-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-3d/2}{2},\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-3d/2}{2}\end{matrix}\right|1\right]

\displaystyle+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu).

(142)

It is possible to further simplify this expression by absorbing a lot of Euler
\Gamma
factors into the generalized hypergeometric function, using the dressed hypergeometric function
{}_{7}\mathcal{F}_{6}
defined in (
106
). This result is shown in (
3.1
).

B.2
Pole structure of the spectral function

In the main text, we use the contour integral and the residue theorem to compute the spectral integral. Therefore, the pole structure of the spectral function
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
in the complex
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}
plane is needed. In this subsection, we present the poles and the corresponding residues.

In the expression (
B.1
) for
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
, the regulated (generalized) hypergeometric function
{}_{7}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\mathrm{F}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{6}
is an entire function of all its
7+6
parameters, and therefore all possible poles come from the Euler
\Gamma
factors and sine function in the denominator. Therefore, we see that there are superficially 5 sets of poles from the Euler Gamma factors, but 3 of them are canceled by the zeros in
{}_{7}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\mathrm{F}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{6}
. As a result, we have two sets of poles at the following positions:

\displaystyle(\text{Set A})

\displaystyle\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=\mathrm{i}d/2\pm 2\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2\mathrm{i}n,

(143)

\displaystyle(\text{Set B})

\displaystyle\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=\mathrm{i}d/2+2\mathrm{i}n.

(144)

The residues at these poles are:

\displaystyle\text{Res}\Big[\mathrm{i}\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\Big]_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=\mathrm{i}d/2\mp 2\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2\mathrm{i}n}=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{8\pi^{d/2}\Gamma(\tfrac{d}{2})}\frac{\sin[\pi(-\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}\mp 2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)]}{\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}

\displaystyle\times\displaystyle\frac{\,{(1+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}\big[(1\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}\big]^{2}(1\pm 2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}}\,}{(1\pm 2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n)_{d-1}},

(145)

\displaystyle\text{Res}\Big[\mathrm{i}\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\Big]_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=\mathrm{i}d/2+2\mathrm{i}n}=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{4\pi^{d/2}\Gamma(\frac{d}{2})}\frac{\sin\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi d}\,}{2}$}}{\sin^{2}\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\displaystyle\frac{\,{[(1+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}]^{2}(1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}(1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}}\,}{(1+2n)_{d-1}}.

(146)

Because there are no poles when
\text{Im}\,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}<0
, the second term of equation (
139
) disappears.

B.3
Spectral function in 3+1 dimensions

The spectral function
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
in 3 spatial dimensions is of special interest to us. As mentioned above,
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
is divergent when
d=3
. The divergence appears as a simple pole, which comes from the factor
\Gamma(2-d)
. Here we want to compute the residue of the spectral function at this simple pole. Therefore, we need to compute
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})/\Gamma(2-d)
, namely, the spectral function with the factor
\Gamma(2-d)
removed.

As shown in (
B.1
), the spectral function
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
consists of two parts, one explicitly in (
B.1
), and the other represented by “
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
.” After removing
\Gamma(2-d)
and then taking
d=3
, the term explicitly displayed in (
B.1
) reads:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8\pi^{3/2}}\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}\tfrac{3}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,-\tfrac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\tfrac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+3/2}{2},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+3/2}{2}\\
1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-1/2}{2},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-1/2}{2}\end{matrix}\right]

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\left.\begin{matrix}-\tfrac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{3/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},-\tfrac{1}{2},-\tfrac{1}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,-\tfrac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+3/2}{2},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+3/2}{2}\\
\tfrac{-1/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-1/2}{2},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-1/2}{2}\end{matrix}\right|1\right].

(147)

To further simplify this expression, we use the following relation
[
93
]

12
12

12

The corresponding formula in
[
93
]
may have a typographical error.
:

\displaystyle{}_{3}\mathrm{F}_{2}\left[\begin{matrix}x,y,z\\
v,w\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]_{n-1}=

\displaystyle~\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}v+w+n-1,x+n,y+n,z+n\\
n,y+z+n,z+x+n,x+y+n\end{matrix}\right]

\displaystyle\times{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}a,\tfrac{1}{2}a+1,w+n-1,v+n-1,x,y,z\\
\tfrac{1}{2}a,v,w,y+z+n,z+x+n,x+y+n\end{matrix}\middle|1\right],

(148)

where
a=x+y+z+n-1
, and

{}_{p}\mathrm{F}_{q}\left[\begin{matrix}a_{1},a_{2},\cdots,a_{p}\\
b_{1},b_{2},\cdots,b_{q}\end{matrix}\middle|z\right]_{N}\equiv\sum_{n=0}^{N}\frac{(a_{1})_{n}(a_{2})_{n}\cdots(a_{q})_{n}}{(b_{1})_{n}(b_{2})_{n}\cdots(b_{q})_{n}}\displaystyle\frac{\,{z^{n}}\,}{n!}.

(149)

With the following parameter assignment:

\displaystyle a=-\frac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,

\displaystyle v=\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-1/2}{2},

\displaystyle w=\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-1/2}{2},

\displaystyle x=-\frac{1}{2},

\displaystyle y=-\frac{1}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,

\displaystyle z=-\frac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},

\displaystyle n=2,

(150)

we can simplify (
B.3
) as

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8\pi^{3/2}}\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}-\tfrac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+3/2}{2},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+3/2}{2}\\
\tfrac{3}{2},\tfrac{3}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-1/2}{2},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-1/2}{2}\end{matrix}\right]{}_{3}\mathrm{F}_{2}\left[\begin{matrix}-\tfrac{1}{2},-\tfrac{1}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,-\frac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\\
\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-1/2}{2},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-1/2}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]_{1}=\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{(4\pi)^{2}}.

(151)

The “
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
” part of
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})/\Gamma(2-d)
in (
B.1
) can be simplified in the same way when
d=3
. The result is again
1/(4\pi)^{2}
. With these results, we see that the whole spectral function
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
diverges at
d\rightarrow 3
as
13
13

13

This result is slightly different from the one given in
[
87
]
, which may have a typographical error.

\lim_{d\rightarrow 3}\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\sim-\frac{1}{8\pi^{2}(3-d)}+\mathcal{O}\Big((3-d)^{0}\Big).

(152)

As is familiar in flat-space loop calculations, the dimensional regularization is often coupled with the modified minimal subtraction scheme (
\overline{\text{MS}}
scheme). This is not a physical renormalization scheme but turns out particularly convenient. Therefore, we define a “renormalized” spectral function
\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\rho\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
under the
\overline{\text{MS}}
scheme as in (
77
). It is possible to work out an explicit expression for
\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\rho\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
by using the two-argument hypergeometric function. The result is rather complicated and uninspiring, but it could be useful for numerical studies. (See the end of App.
C.1
for an example.) Here we present this result for completeness. To this end, it is useful to define the following shorthand:

\displaystyle\vartheta(a)\equiv\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{a}{}_{7}\Theta_{6}^{(1)}\left[\begin{matrix}1,1\\
a_{1}+1\end{matrix}\middle|\begin{matrix}a,a_{1}+1,\cdots,a_{7}+1\\
2,b_{1}+1,\cdots,b_{6}+1\end{matrix}\middle|z,z\right],

(153)

where the function
\displaystyle{}_{p}\Theta_{q}^{(1)}
is defined in (
A
), and the parameters
(a_{1},\cdots,a_{7},b_{1},\cdots,b_{6})
are the same as the ones appearing in the
{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}
function in (
B.1
). Then:

\displaystyle\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\rho\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})=

\displaystyle~\bigg[\frac{1}{16\pi^{2}}\Big\{-1-\cot(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)+\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}\log(16\pi)+\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}\Big[\psi(\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{2}$}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)-\psi(-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})

\displaystyle-\psi(\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)+\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}\psi(\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+3/2}\,}{2}$})+\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}\psi(\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+3/2}\,}{2}$})+\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{2}$}(\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-1/2}\,}{2}$})+\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{2}$}(\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-1/2}\,}{2}$})\Big]\Big\}

\displaystyle-\displaystyle\frac{\,{(\tfrac{3/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2})(-\tfrac{1}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)(\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+3/2}{2})(\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+3/2}{2})}\,}{8\pi^{3/2}}\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}\tfrac{3}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\tfrac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
2-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\right]

\displaystyle\times\Big\{-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}\vartheta(-1/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}\vartheta(\mbox{$\frac{\,{3/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$})-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}\vartheta(-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$})-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}\vartheta(-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)

\displaystyle-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}\vartheta(-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})+\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}\vartheta(\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+3/2}\,}{2}$})+\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}\vartheta(\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+3/2}\,}{2}$})+\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}\vartheta(\mbox{$\frac{\,{-1/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$})

\displaystyle+\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{4}$}\vartheta(\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-1/2}\,}{2}$})+\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{4}$}\vartheta(\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-1/2}\,}{2}$})\Big\}\bigg]+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu).

(154)

B.4
More on the
\mathbf{\Pi}
function

At several places in the final result for the loop seed integral, the spectral function
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
appears through the combination
\Pi_{\nu^{\prime},d}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\equiv\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(-\mathrm{i}\nu^{\prime})-\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(+\mathrm{i}\nu^{\prime})
where
\nu^{\prime}=\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}
is an integer or half-integer. In this appendix, we derive an alternative expression for
\Pi_{\nu^{\prime},d}
for general
d
which is more convenient for some purposes. We also derive a closed form formula for
\Pi_{\nu^{\prime},d}
for
d=3
, and show that
\Pi_{n,d}=0
when
n
is an integer.

Our starting point here is the original expression for the spectral function (
3.1
). We apply the following connecting formula for the generalized hypergeometric function
{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}

[
94
]
:

\displaystyle{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{A}\\
\mathbf{B}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]=\Gamma\bigg[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{C}\\
\mathbf{D}\end{matrix}\bigg]-\Gamma\bigg[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{E}\\
\mathbf{F}\end{matrix}\bigg]{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{G}\\
\mathbf{H}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right],

(155)

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}=

\displaystyle~\{a,1+\tfrac{a}{2},b,c,e,f,g\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{B}=

\displaystyle~\{\tfrac{a}{2},1+a-b,1+a-c,1+a-e,1+a-f,1+a-g\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{C}=

\displaystyle~\{1+a-c,1+a-e,1+a-f,1+a-g,b+c-a,b+e-a,b+f-a,b+g-a\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}=

\displaystyle~\{1+a,b-a,1+a-e-f,1+a-c-f,1+a-c-e,

\displaystyle~~\,1+a-c-g,1+a-e-g,1+a-f-g\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}=

\displaystyle~\{1+2b-a,b+c-a,b+e-a,b+f-a,b+g-a,a-b,

\displaystyle~~\,1+a-c,1+a-e,1+a-f,1+a-g\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{F}=

\displaystyle~\{1+b-c,1+b-e,1+b-f,1+b-g,b-a,1+a,c,e,f,g\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}=

\displaystyle~\{2b-a,1+b-\tfrac{a}{2},b,b+c-a,b+e-a,b+f-a,b+g-a\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{H}=

\displaystyle~\{b-\tfrac{a}{2},1+b-a,1+b-c,1+b-e,1+b-f,1+b-g\}.

(156)

The formula (
155
) has the effect of transforming the
{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}
function in
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
into the
{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}
function in
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
. Therefore, we apply it to the
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
term in the
\Pi
function, through the following assignment of parameters:

\displaystyle a=1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\mp\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,

\displaystyle b=1-\tfrac{d}{2},

\displaystyle c=1-\tfrac{d}{2}\mp\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,

\displaystyle e=1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},

\displaystyle f=\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\mp 2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2}{2},

\displaystyle g=\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+d/2}{2}.

(157)

Meanwhile, we keep the
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
term in the
\Pi
function intact. As a result, the various generalized hypergeometric functions in the
\Pi
function can be combined. With some further simplifications, we get the following result:

\displaystyle\Pi_{\nu^{\prime},d}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8\pi^{d/2}}\bigg[\frac{\cot[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)]\sin[\pi(\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-\nu^{\prime})]\sin[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(-\nu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2)]}{\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\sin[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(\nu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2)]}

\displaystyle-\frac{\cot[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(d+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)]\sin[\pi(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-\nu^{\prime})]\sin[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(-\nu^{\prime}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2)]}{\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\sin[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(\nu^{\prime}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2)]}\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}1-\tfrac{d}{2}-\nu^{\prime},\tfrac{-\nu^{\prime}+d/2}{2},\tfrac{-\nu^{\prime}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2}{2},\tfrac{-\nu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2}{2},1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\nu^{\prime},\tfrac{\nu^{\prime}+d/2}{2}\\
\tfrac{-\nu^{\prime}+2-d/2}{2},\tfrac{d}{2},\tfrac{\nu^{\prime}+2-d/2}{2},\tfrac{-\nu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2-d/2}{2},\tfrac{-\nu^{\prime}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2-d/2}{2}\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg\{\frac{1}{8\pi^{d/2}}\bigg[\frac{\cot[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(-d+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)]\sin[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(d+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)]}{\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}-\frac{\cos[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(d+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)]}{\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Big[2-d,\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1-\tfrac{d}{2}-\nu^{\prime},2-\tfrac{d}{2}-\nu^{\prime}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{-\nu^{\prime}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2}{2},\tfrac{-\nu^{\prime}+d/2}{2},\tfrac{1-d/2-\nu^{\prime}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\times{}_{7}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\mathrm{F}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}1-\tfrac{d}{2}-\nu^{\prime}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{3-d/2-\nu^{\prime}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},1-\tfrac{d}{2},1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1-\tfrac{d}{2}-\nu^{\prime},\tfrac{-\nu^{\prime}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2}{2},\tfrac{-\nu^{\prime}+d/2}{2}\\
1-\nu^{\prime}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1-\nu^{\prime},1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{4-\nu^{\prime}-3d/2}{2},\tfrac{4-\nu^{\prime}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-3d/2}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]

\displaystyle+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\bigg\}.

(158)

We are particularly interested in the case of
d=3
. For the two terms in (
B.4
), the
d\rightarrow 3
limit of the first term (without
{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}
) is straightforward, and the result is:

\displaystyle-\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{8\pi}\displaystyle\frac{\,{\sin(\pi\nu^{\prime})}\,}{\sin(\pi\nu^{\prime})-\cosh(2\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}-\tfrac{1}{4}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\nu^{\prime}}\,}{2}$},-\tfrac{1}{4}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\nu^{\prime}}\,}{2}$},\tfrac{-\nu^{\prime}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+3/2}{2},\tfrac{-\nu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+3/2}{2}\\
\tfrac{1}{4}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\nu^{\prime}}\,}{2}$},\tfrac{1}{4}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\nu^{\prime}}\,}{2}$},\tfrac{-\nu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1/2}{2},\tfrac{-\nu^{\prime}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1/2}{2}\\
\end{matrix}\Bigg].

(159)

More care is needed to take the
d\rightarrow 3
limit of the second term in (
B.4
), namely the whole term within the curly bracket. Due to the factor
\Gamma(2-d)
, this term is seemingly divergent; However, the divergence is canceled by another divergent term in the “
(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)
” term. To see this, we note that we can isolate part of terms in the curly bracket of (
B.4
) that is identical to (
B.3
) upon the replacement
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
and
\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\rightarrow-\nu^{\prime}
. Therefore, we can use the same treatment for (
B.3
) to compute the
d\rightarrow 3
limit of this curly-bracket term. Comparing with (
151
), we see that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8\pi^{d/2}}\Gamma\Big[\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1-\tfrac{d}{2}-\nu^{\prime},2-\tfrac{d}{2}-\nu^{\prime}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{-\nu^{\prime}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2}{2},\tfrac{-\nu^{\prime}+d/2}{2},\tfrac{1-d/2-\nu^{\prime}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\times{}_{7}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\mathrm{F}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}1-\tfrac{d}{2}-\nu^{\prime}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{3-d/2-\nu^{\prime}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},1-\tfrac{d}{2},1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1-\tfrac{d}{2}-\nu^{\prime},\tfrac{-\nu^{\prime}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2}{2},\tfrac{-\nu^{\prime}+d/2}{2}\\
1-\nu^{\prime}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1-\nu^{\prime},1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{4-\nu^{\prime}-3d/2}{2},\tfrac{4-\nu^{\prime}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-3d/2}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]

\displaystyle=\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{(4\pi)^{2}}.

(160)

Therefore, the whole term inside the curly bracket in (
B.4
) vanishes when
d=3
:

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{\Gamma(2-d)}\,}{(4\pi)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{\cot[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(-d+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)]\sin[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(d+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)]}{\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}-\frac{\cos[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(d+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)]}{\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\bigg]+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)=0.

(161)

Therefore, we have got the following explicit expression for
\Pi_{n,3}
:

\displaystyle\Pi_{n,3}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)=\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{8\pi}\displaystyle\frac{\,{\sin(\pi n)}\,}{\cosh(2\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)-\sin(\pi n)}\displaystyle\frac{\,{\big(\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)_{\frac{1}{2}}\big(\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)_{\frac{1}{2}}}\,}{(-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$})_{\frac{1}{2}}(-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$})_{\frac{1}{2}}}.~~~~(n\in\mathbb{R})

(162)

Note that this result applies for general
n
, which is not required to be an integer. For our computation of loop seed integral in 3 spatial dimensions, it is worth noting that
\Pi_{n,3}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)=0
when
n
is an integer. The case of half-integer
n
is also useful but is a bit complicated. First, when
n=1/2
,
\Pi_{1/2,3}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)
is finite:

\Pi_{1/2,3}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)=\displaystyle\frac{\,{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{4\pi\sinh(2\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}.

(163)

Next, when
n=2k+1/2
with
k=1,2,\cdots
,
\Pi_{n,3}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)=0
. Finally, when
n=2k+3/2
with
k=0,1,2,\cdots
,
\Pi_{n,3}
is divergent like a simple pole.

It is also straightforward to find an expression for the
\Xi_{n,3}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)
function defined in (
46
). This time we require
n
to be an integer. The result is:

\displaystyle\Xi_{n,3}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)=\displaystyle\frac{\,{(-1)^{n}}\,}{8}\displaystyle\frac{\,{\big(\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)_{\frac{1}{2}}\big(\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)_{\frac{1}{2}}}\,}{(-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$})_{\frac{1}{2}}(-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$})_{\frac{1}{2}}}\text{sech}(2\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu).~~~~(n\in\mathbb{Z})

(164)

Appendix C
Asymptotic Behavior of the Spectral Function

In several places of the main text, we need the asymptotic behavior of the spectral function
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
when either
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
or
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}
is large. We examine these two limits in this appendix.

C.1
Large
\bm{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
limit

The spectral function
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
as given in (
3.1
) contains two terms, each of which is a product of a generalized hypergeometric function
{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}
and many Euler
\Gamma
and trigonometric functions. To find the large
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
limit of this function, therefore, we should take the large
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
limit of all these factors.

It turns out that the leading result of the large
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
limit is canceled between the explicitly displayed term and the
(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)
term in (
3.1
). To find the leading nonvanishing result, therefore, we need to know the first two leading terms of all these functions in the large
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
expansion. Below, we discuss them in turn.

Large argument limit of the
\mathbf{\Gamma}
ratio.

First, we shall need the large
z
limit of the ratio of two Euler
\Gamma
functions
[
94
]
:

\displaystyle\frac{\Gamma(z+a)}{\Gamma(z+b)}\sim z^{a-b}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{z^{n}}{a-b\choose k}\mathrm{B}_{k}^{(a-b+1)}(a),

(165)

which holds so long as
z
is away from the negative real axis. Here
\mathrm{B}_{n}^{(l)}
are generalized Bernoulli polynomials
[
94
]
. We shall need the first two of them:

\displaystyle\mathrm{G}_{0}(a,b)=1,

\displaystyle\mathrm{G}_{1}(a,b)=\frac{1}{2}(a-b)(a+b-1).

(166)

With (
165
), we can easily find the following expansion for the products and ratios of Euler
\Gamma
functions in (
3.1
):

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{8\pi^{d/2}}\displaystyle\frac{\,{\cos[\pi(-\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)]}\,}{\sin(-\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2-d,\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},2-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2}{2},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+d/2}{2}\\
1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-3d/2}{2},\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-3d/2}{2}\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathrm{i}}{8\pi^{d/2}}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2-d,1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+d/2}{2}\\
1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-3d/2}{2}\end{matrix}\Bigg]\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{d-2}+\mathcal{O}\big(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{d-4}\big).

(167)

As mentioned above, we need to keep the first two orders in
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
in the expansion. However, it turns out that the next-to-leading order term, namely the
\mathcal{O}({\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{d-3}})
term, vanishes identically. Therefore, the omitted terms in (
C.1
) start from
\mathcal{O}({\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{d-4}})
.

Large parameter limit of the generalized hypergeometric function.

It is not so trivial to get the large
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
expansion of the generalized hypergeometric function in (
3.1
). Here we provide some details. To avoid unnecessarily lengthy expressions, we introduce the following four groups of parameters:

\displaystyle\mathbf{a}=

\displaystyle~\Big\{1-\tfrac{d}{2},1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+d/2}{2}\Big\},

(168)

\displaystyle\mathbf{b}(\zeta)=

\displaystyle~\Big\{1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\tfrac{\mathrm{i}}{\zeta},\tfrac{3-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}/\zeta}{2},1-\tfrac{d}{2}-\tfrac{\mathrm{i}}{\zeta},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}/\zeta+d/2}{2}\Big\},

(169)

\displaystyle\mathbf{c}=

\displaystyle~\Big\{1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-3d/2}{2}\Big\},

(170)

\displaystyle\mathbf{d}(\zeta)=

\displaystyle~\Big\{\tfrac{1-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}/\zeta}{2},1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\tfrac{\mathrm{i}}{\zeta},1-\tfrac{\mathrm{i}}{\zeta},\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}/\zeta-3d/2}{2}\Big\}.

(171)

Here and below, for a group of parameters
\mathbf{a}=\{a_{1},\cdots,a_{p}\}
, we use the shorthand notation
(\mathbf{a})_{\ell}\equiv(a_{1})_{\ell}\cdots(a_{p})_{\ell}
, and
(\mathbf{a}+n)_{\ell}\equiv(a_{1}+n)_{\ell}\cdots(a_{p}+n)_{\ell}
. The group labels such as
\mathbf{a}
and
\mathbf{b}(\zeta)
can also freely appear in the hypergeometric functions or the products of Euler
\Gamma
functions, which should be self-explanatory.

Now, if we take
\zeta=1/\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
, then the hypergeometric function in (
3.1
) can be written and expanded in the following way around
\zeta=0
:

\displaystyle{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b}(\zeta)\\
\mathbf{c},\mathbf{d}(\zeta)\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]=\lim_{\zeta\rightarrow 0}{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b}(\zeta)\\
\mathbf{c},\mathbf{d}(\zeta)\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]+\zeta\lim_{\zeta\rightarrow 0}\displaystyle\frac{\,{\partial}\,}{\partial\zeta}{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b}(\zeta)\\
\mathbf{c},\mathbf{d}(\zeta)\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]+\mathcal{O}({\zeta^{2}}).

(172)

First, consider the
\zeta^{0}
-term:

\displaystyle\lim_{\zeta\rightarrow 0}{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b}(\zeta)\\
\mathbf{c},\mathbf{d}(\zeta)\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]={}_{3}\mathrm{F}_{2}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{a}\\
\mathbf{c}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]=\pi^{1/2}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-3d/2}{2},1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\tfrac{3+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-d/2}{2}\\
\tfrac{3-d}{2},\tfrac{2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-d/2}{2},2-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\tfrac{1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+d/2}{2}\end{matrix}\Bigg].

(173)

Then, consider the
\zeta^{1}
-term. Here we need to take derivatives of the hypergeometric function with respect to its parameters, as summarized in (
107
) and (
108
). To save some space, we again use the following shorthand:

\displaystyle\vartheta(a)\equiv\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{a}{}_{7}\Theta_{6}^{(1)}\left[\begin{matrix}1,1\\
a_{1}+1\end{matrix}\middle|\begin{matrix}a,a_{1}+1,\cdots,a_{7}+1\\
2,b_{1}+1,\cdots,b_{6}+1\end{matrix}\middle|z,z\right],

(174)

where the parameters
(a_{1},\cdots,a_{7},b_{1},\cdots,b_{6})
are given by
(\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b}(\zeta),\mathbf{c},\mathbf{d}(\zeta))
, respectively. Then,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{\zeta}}{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b}(\zeta)\\
\mathbf{c},\mathbf{d}(\zeta)\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}}\,}{\zeta^{2}}\displaystyle\frac{\,{(\mathbf{a})_{1}(\mathbf{b}(\zeta))_{1}}\,}{(\mathbf{c})_{1}(\mathbf{d}(\zeta))_{1}}.\Big[\vartheta\Big(1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\tfrac{\mathrm{i}}{\zeta}\Big)+\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}\vartheta\Big(\tfrac{3-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}/\zeta}{2}\Big)+\vartheta\Big(1-\tfrac{d}{2}-\tfrac{\mathrm{i}}{\zeta}\Big)+\vartheta\Big(\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}/\zeta+d/2}{2}\Big)

\displaystyle-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}\vartheta\Big(\tfrac{1-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}/\zeta}{2}\Big)-\vartheta\Big(1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\tfrac{\mathrm{i}}{\zeta}\Big)-\vartheta\Big(1-\tfrac{\mathrm{i}}{\zeta}\Big)-\Big(\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}/\zeta-3d/2}{2}\Big)\Big].

(175)

Now, we use the definition of the two-argument hypergeometric function
{}_{p}\Theta_{q}^{(1)}
in (
A
). Then we get

\displaystyle\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{\zeta}}{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b}(\zeta)\\
\mathbf{c},\mathbf{d}(\zeta)\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]

\displaystyle=
