\displaystyle~\frac{\mathrm{i}}{\zeta^{2}}\frac{(\mathbf{a})(\mathbf{b}(\zeta))}{(\mathbf{c})(\mathbf{d}(\zeta))}\sum_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}=0}^{\infty}\frac{(1)_{\ell_{1}}(1)_{\ell_{2}}(\mathbf{a}+1)_{\ell_{1}+\ell_{2}}(\mathbf{b}(\zeta)+1)_{\ell_{1}+\ell_{2}}}{\ell_{1}!\ell_{2}!(2)_{\ell_{1}+\ell_{2}}(\mathbf{c}+1)_{\ell_{1}+\ell_{2}}(\mathbf{d}(\zeta)+1)_{\ell_{1}+\ell_{2}}}\bigg[\frac{(2-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}/\zeta)_{\ell_{1}-1}}{(2-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}/\zeta)_{\ell_{1}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{((5-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}/\zeta)/2)_{\ell_{1}-1}}{2((5-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}/\zeta)/2)_{\ell_{1}}}+\frac{(2-d/2-\mathrm{i}/\zeta)_{\ell_{1}-1}}{(2-d/2-\mathrm{i}/\zeta)_{\ell_{1}}}+\frac{((2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}/\zeta+d/2)/2)_{\ell_{1}-1}}{((2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}/\zeta+d/2)/2)_{\ell_{1}}}

\displaystyle-\frac{((3-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}/\zeta)/2)_{\ell_{1}-1}}{2((3-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}/\zeta)/2)_{\ell_{1}}}-\frac{(2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}/\zeta)_{\ell_{1}-1}}{(2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}/\zeta)_{\ell_{1}}}-\frac{(2-\mathrm{i}/\zeta)_{\ell_{1}-1}}{(2-\mathrm{i}/\zeta)_{\ell_{1}}}

\displaystyle-\frac{((6+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}/\zeta-3d/2)/2)_{\ell_{1}-1}}{((6+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}/\zeta-3d/2)/2)_{\ell_{1}}}\bigg].

(176)

With a bit of simplification, the above expression can be rewritten in the following way:

\displaystyle\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{\zeta}}{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b}(\zeta)\\
\mathbf{c},\mathbf{d}(\zeta)\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]=\frac{\mathrm{i}}{\zeta^{2}}\frac{(\mathbf{a})(\mathbf{b}(\zeta))}{(\mathbf{c})(\mathbf{d}(\zeta))}\sum_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}=0}^{\infty}\frac{(\mathbf{a}+1)_{\ell_{1}+\ell_{2}}(\mathbf{b}(\zeta)+1)_{\ell_{1}+\ell_{2}}}{(2)_{\ell_{1}+\ell_{2}}(\mathbf{c}+1)_{\ell_{1}+\ell_{2}}(\mathbf{d}(\zeta)+1)_{\ell_{1}+\ell_{2}}}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\frac{d/2}{(1-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}/\zeta+\ell_{1})(1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}/\zeta+\ell_{1})}+\frac{d/2}{(1-d/2-\mathrm{i}/\zeta+\ell_{1})(1-\mathrm{i}/\zeta+\ell_{1})}

\displaystyle+\frac{2-d}{((\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}/\zeta+d/2)/2+\ell_{1})((4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}/\zeta-3d/2)/2+\ell_{1})}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2((3-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}/\zeta)/2+\ell_{1})((1-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}/\zeta)/2+\ell_{1})}\bigg].

(177)

Now, if we directly take the limit
\zeta\rightarrow 0
, the above result will reduce to a “
0\times\infty
”-type expression. To regulate this singular behavior, we introduce a positive infinitesimal parameter
\epsilon
through the following redefinition of the parameter sets:

\displaystyle\mathbf{b}(\zeta)\rightarrow\mathbf{b}_{\epsilon}(\zeta)=

\displaystyle\Big\{1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\tfrac{\mathrm{i}}{\zeta}-\epsilon,\tfrac{3-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}/\zeta}{2},1-\tfrac{d}{2}-\tfrac{\mathrm{i}}{\zeta},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}/\zeta+d/2}{2}\Big\},

(178)

\displaystyle\mathbf{c}\rightarrow\mathbf{c}_{\epsilon}=

\displaystyle\Big\{1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+\epsilon,\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-3d/2}{2}\Big\}.

(179)

With this
\epsilon
-regulator, the
\zeta\rightarrow 0
limit can be properly taken:

\displaystyle\lim_{\zeta\rightarrow 0}\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{\zeta}}{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b}(\zeta)\\
\mathbf{c},\mathbf{d}(\zeta)\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]=\lim_{\epsilon\rightarrow 0^{+}}\lim_{\zeta\rightarrow 0}\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{\zeta}}{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b}_{\epsilon}(\zeta)\\
\mathbf{c}_{\epsilon},\mathbf{d}(\zeta)\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\lim_{\epsilon\rightarrow 0^{+}}\mathrm{i}\epsilon\frac{(\mathbf{a})}{(\mathbf{c}_{\epsilon})}\sum_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}=0}^{\infty}\frac{(\mathbf{a}+1)_{\ell_{1}+\ell_{2}}}{(2)_{\ell_{1}+\ell_{2}}(\mathbf{c}_{\epsilon}+1)_{\ell_{1}+\ell_{2}}}=-\lim_{\epsilon\rightarrow 0^{+}}\mathrm{i}\epsilon\frac{(\mathbf{a})}{(\mathbf{c}_{\epsilon})}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{(\ell+1)(\mathbf{a}+1)_{\ell}}{(2)_{\ell}(\mathbf{c}_{\epsilon}+1)_{\ell}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\lim_{\epsilon\rightarrow 0^{+}}\mathrm{i}\epsilon\frac{(\mathbf{a})}{(\mathbf{c}_{\epsilon})}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{(\mathbf{a}+1)_{\ell}}{\ell!(\mathbf{c}_{\epsilon}+1)_{\ell}}=-\lim_{\epsilon\rightarrow 0^{+}}\mathrm{i}\epsilon\frac{(\mathbf{a})}{(\mathbf{c}_{\epsilon})}{}_{3}\mathrm{F}_{2}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{a}+1\\
\mathbf{c}_{\epsilon}+1\end{matrix}\middle|1\right].

(180)

It is possible to further simplify this result by applying a generalization of Dixon’s theorem
[
93
]
:

{}_{3}\mathrm{F}_{2}\left[\begin{matrix}a,b,c\\
e,f\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]=\Gamma\bigg[\begin{matrix}e,f,s\\
a,b+s,c+s\end{matrix}\bigg]{}_{3}\mathrm{F}_{2}\left[\begin{matrix}e-a,f-a,s\\
s+b,s+c\end{matrix}\middle|1\right],

(181)

where
s=e+f-a-b-c
. Comparing the
{}_{3}\mathrm{F}_{2}
function in the last expression of (
C.1
) with (
181
), together with (
168
) and (
179
), we see that we can make the following assignment:

\displaystyle a=2-\displaystyle\frac{\,{d}\,}{2},

\displaystyle b=2-\displaystyle\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},

\displaystyle c=\displaystyle\frac{\,{2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+d/2}\,}{2},

\displaystyle e=2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+\epsilon,

\displaystyle f=\displaystyle\frac{\,{6+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-3d/2}\,}{2},

(182)

then we have
s=\epsilon
. Using (
181
), we finally get the coefficient of the
\mathcal{O}({\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu})
term in the large
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
expansion:

\lim_{\zeta\rightarrow 0}\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{\zeta}}{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b}(\zeta)\\
\mathbf{c},\mathbf{d}(\zeta)\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]=-\mathrm{i}\Gamma\bigg[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{c}\\
\mathbf{a}\end{matrix}\bigg].

(183)

Combining the above results, we see that the hypergeometric function has the following asymptotic behavior in the large
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
limit:

\displaystyle{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b}(\zeta)\\
\mathbf{c},\mathbf{d}(\zeta)\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]={}_{3}\mathrm{F}_{2}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{a}\\
\mathbf{c}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]-\frac{\mathrm{i}}{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Gamma\bigg[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{c}\\
\mathbf{a}\end{matrix}\bigg]+\mathcal{O}\Big(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{-2}\Big).

(184)

Final result.

Therefore, the asymptotic expression of spectral density is

\displaystyle\lim_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow\infty}\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\sim

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4\pi^{d/2}}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2-d,1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+d/2}{2},\mathbf{c}\\
1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-3d/2}{2},\mathbf{a}\end{matrix}\Bigg]\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{d-3}+\mathcal{O}\Big(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{d-4}\Big).

(185)

After further simplifications, we finally get:

\displaystyle\lim_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow\infty}\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\sim

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{(4\pi)^{(d+1)/2}}\Gamma\bigg(\frac{3-d}{2}\bigg)\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{d-3}+\mathcal{O}\Big(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{d-4}\Big).

(186)

In particular, when
d\rightarrow 3
, we find that the renormalized spectral function with
\overline{\text{MS}}
, as defined in (
77
), has the following asymptotic behavior in the large-mass limit:

\lim_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow\infty}\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\rho\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\sim\frac{1}{16\pi^{2}}\log(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2})+\mathcal{O}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{-1}).

(187)

Numerical fit of the spectral function at
\bm{\mathcal{O}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{-2})}
.

It is satisfactory to see that the leading order result (
187
) of the spectral function in the large
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
limit agrees with what we would expect from the flat space. (See App.
F
.) However, we know that this logarithmic dependence on
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
is from the 1-loop renormalization, and can be subtracted by choosing the renormalization scale properly. Therefore, it would useful to have the behavior of the spectral function at the next order in
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
. The flat-space result suggests that the
\mathcal{O}({\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{-1}})
contribution should be zero, and the first nonvanishing subleading contribution should come from
\mathcal{O}({\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{-2}})
. To get the coefficient of this order requires us to take the derivative of parameters of
{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}
function thrice, which is analytically nontrivial. Fortunately, it is easy to do a numerical fit from the explicit expression of
\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\rho\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
in (
B.3
). For large
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
(and not too large
\nu^{\prime}
), we find the following expression a perfect fit:

\lim_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow\infty}\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\rho\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(-\mathrm{i}{\nu^{\prime}})\sim\frac{1}{16\pi^{2}}\bigg[\log(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2})+\Big(\displaystyle\frac{\,{2\nu^{\prime}}\,}{5\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Big)^{2}\bigg].

(188)

In Fig.
6
, we show the full spectral function with the
\overline{\text{MS}}
scheme against several analytical approximations. We see good asymptotic agreements between the full spectral function
\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\rho\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(-\mathrm{i}{\nu^{\prime}})
, evaluated with the explicit formula (
B.3
), and the analytical approximations given in (
188
).

Figure 6:
The renormalized spectral function
\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\rho\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(-\mathrm{i}{\nu^{\prime}})
in (
B.3
) and its approximations in the large
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
limit. The left panel compares the leading (logarithmic) order behavior for a range of
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
, the middle panel compares the subleading (inverse quadratic) order behavior, and the right panel shows the dependence on
\nu^{\prime}
for a fixed value of
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu=30
.

C.2
Large
\bm{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}
limit

The large
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}
limit of the spectral function
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
can be worked out similarly. We shall be brief. First, the large
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}
limit of the
{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}
function can be worked out as:

\displaystyle~{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{3-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},1-\tfrac{d}{2},1-\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2}{2},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+d/2}{2}\\
\tfrac{1-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-3d/2}{2},\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-3d/2}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle~{}_{2}\mathrm{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}1-\tfrac{d}{2},1-\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]+\mathcal{O}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime-1}).

(189)

Next, the pre-factors of the
{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}
function in (
3.1
) have the following asymptotic behavior for large
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}
:

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{8\pi^{d/2}}\displaystyle\frac{\,{\cos[\frac{\pi}{2}(-d+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)]}\,}{\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2-d,\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},2-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2}{2},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+d/2}{2}\\
1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-3d/2}{2},\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-3d/2}{2}\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{2^{2d-1}\pi^{d/2}}\displaystyle\frac{\,{\cos[\frac{\pi}{2}(-d+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)]}\,}{\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2-d,\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\Bigg](\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})^{d-3}+\mathcal{O}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime d-4}).

(190)

Combining the above two results, we conclude that the spectral density
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\sim(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})^{d-3}
when
|\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}|\gg 1
.

Appendix D
Tree Seed Integral in Arbitrary Dimensions

The 4-point function with tree-level massive exchange is one of the ingredients for our bootstrap program of 1-loop correlators. To implement the dimensional regularization, we need the result for the tree-level correlator in general
d
spatial dimensions. In this appendix, we compute the tree seed integral defined in (
25
) for arbitrary
(p_{1},p_{2},d)
.

We shall use the partial Mellin-Barnes representation to compute the integral and we will compute the four terms in the summation in (
25
) one by one. The computation is largely in parallel with the one given in
[
72
]
, in which the readers can find more details about the method. Here we shall be brief.

The four terms in the summation in (
25
) are given by: (We omit the label for the mass
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
.)

\mathcal{I}_{\mathsf{ab}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})\equiv-\mathsf{ab}\,k_{s}^{d+2+p_{1}+p_{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}{\mathrm{d}}\tau_{1}{\mathrm{d}}\tau_{2}\left(-\tau_{1}\right)^{p_{1}}\left(-\tau_{2}\right)^{p_{2}}\text{e}^{\mathrm{i}\mathsf{a}k_{12}\tau_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mathsf{b}k_{34}\tau_{2}}D_{\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}}\left(k_{s};\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\right).

(191)

In
d
spatial dimensions, the Mellin-Barnes representation for the two homogeneous propagators in (
5
) are:

\displaystyle D_{\gtrless}\left(k_{s};\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\right)=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{4\pi}\int_{-\mathrm{i}\infty}^{+\mathrm{i}\infty}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s_{1}}{2\pi\mathrm{i}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s_{2}}{2\pi\mathrm{i}}\text{e}^{\pm\mathrm{i}\pi\left(s_{1}-s_{2}\right)}\Big(\frac{k_{s}}{2}\Big)^{-2s_{12}}\left(-\tau_{1}\right)^{-2s_{1}+d/2}\left(-\tau_{2}\right)^{-2s_{2}+d/2}

\displaystyle~\times\Gamma\bigg[s_{1}-\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},s_{1}+\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},s_{2}-\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},s_{2}+\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2}\bigg].

(192)

Together with (
3
) and (
4
), we can finish the time integrals in the seed integral. For the two opposite-sign integrals
\mathcal{I}_{\mp\pm}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\mp\pm}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})=\frac{1}{4\pi}\text{e}^{\pm\mathrm{i}\pi\left(p_{1}-p_{2}\right)/2}r_{1}^{d/2+1+p_{1}}r_{2}^{d/2+1+p_{2}}\int_{-\mathrm{i}\infty}^{+\mathrm{i}\infty}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s_{1}}{2\pi\mathrm{i}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s_{2}}{2\pi\mathrm{i}}\left(\frac{r_{1}}{2}\right)^{-2s_{1}}\left(\frac{r_{2}}{2}\right)^{-2s_{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Big[s_{1}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},s_{1}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},s_{2}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},s_{2}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},-2s_{1}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+1+p_{1},-2s_{2}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+1+p_{2}\Big].

(193)

The two same-sign integrals
\mathcal{I}_{\pm\pm}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
can be separated into two parts, one called the “factorized” (F) and the other called the “time-ordered” (TO): (See
[
72
]
for the details.)

\mathcal{I}_{\pm\pm}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})=\mathcal{I}_{\pm\pm,\text{F},>}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})+\mathcal{I}_{\pm\pm,\text{TO},>}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2}).~~~~(r_{1}<r_{2})

(194)

More explicitly,

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\pm\pm,\text{F},>}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})=\frac{\text{e}^{-\mathrm{i}\pi(p_{12}+d)/2}}{4\pi}r_{1}^{d/2+1+p_{1}}r_{2}^{d/2+1+p_{2}}\int_{-\mathrm{i}\infty}^{\mathrm{i}\infty}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s_{1}}{2\pi\mathrm{i}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s_{2}}{2\pi\mathrm{i}}\text{e}^{2\mathrm{i}\pi s_{1}}\Big(\frac{r_{1}}{2}\Big)^{-2s_{1}}\Big(\frac{r_{2}}{2}\Big)^{-2s_{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Big[s_{1}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},s_{1}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},s_{2}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},s_{2}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},-2s_{1}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+1+p_{1},-2s_{2}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+1+p_{2}\Big],

(195)

and,

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\pm\pm,\text{TO},>}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})=-\frac{\text{e}^{\mp\mathrm{i}\pi(p_{12}+d)/2}}{4\pi}r_{1}^{d+2+p_{12}}\int_{-\mathrm{i}\infty}^{\mathrm{i}\infty}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s_{1}}{2\pi\mathrm{i}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s_{2}}{2\pi\mathrm{i}}\Gamma\Big[s_{1}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},s_{1}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},s_{2}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},s_{2}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}\Big]

\displaystyle\times(\text{e}^{\pm 2\mathrm{i}\pi s_{1}}-\text{e}^{\pm 2\mathrm{i}\pi s_{2}})\Big(\frac{r_{1}}{2}\Big)^{-2s_{12}}{}_{2}\mathcal{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}-2s_{2}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+p_{2}+1,-2s_{12}+d+p_{12}+2\\
-2s_{2}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+p_{2}+2\end{matrix}\middle|-\displaystyle\frac{\,{r_{1}}\,}{r_{2}}\right].

(196)

To finish the integration over the Mellin variables
s_{1}
and
s_{2}
, we close the contours from the left, since we are assuming
r_{1}<r_{2}<1
. Then, the enclosed poles can be classified into two groups:

\displaystyle s_{1}=-n_{1}\mp\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},\

\displaystyle s_{2}=-n_{2}\pm\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},

(197)

\displaystyle s_{1}=-n_{1}\mp\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},\

\displaystyle s_{2}=-n_{2}\mp\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2}.

(198)

Using the residue theorem for all these poles, and also summing over the four integrals in (
191
), we can find the final result for the tree seed integral. As shown in (
28
), the tree seed integral can be broken into three parts:

\sum_{\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b}=\pm}\mathcal{I}_{\mathsf{ab}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})=\mathcal{I}_{\text{NL},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})+\mathcal{I}_{\text{L},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})+\mathcal{I}_{\text{BG},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2}).

(199)

The three pieces are respectively given by:

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\text{NL},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{4\pi}r_{1}^{d/2+1+p_{1}}r_{2}^{d/2+1+p_{2}}\sum_{n_{1},n_{2}=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{n_{12}}}{n_{1}!n_{2}!}\Big[\text{e}^{\mathrm{i}\pi\overline{p}_{12}/2}+\text{e}^{\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-\mathrm{i}\pi(p_{12}+d)/2}\Big]\Big(\frac{r_{1}}{2}\Big)^{2n_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Big(\frac{r_{2}}{2}\Big)^{2n_{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Big[2n_{1}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+1+p_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,2n_{2}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+1+p_{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,-n_{1}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,-n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\Big]+\text{c.c.}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{2\pi}\Big[\cos\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi\overline{p}_{12}}\,}{2}$}+\cos\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi(2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{12}+d)}\,}{2}$}\Big]r_{1}^{d/2+1+p_{1}}r_{2}^{d/2+1+p_{2}}\Big(\frac{r_{1}r_{2}}{4}\Big)^{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Big[\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+1+p_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+1+p_{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\Big]

\displaystyle\times{}_{2}F_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}\mbox{$\frac{\,{d+2p_{1}+2}\,}{4}$}+\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},\mbox{$\frac{\,{d+2p_{1}+4}\,}{4}$}+\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2}\\
1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\middle|r_{1}^{2}\right]{}_{2}F_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}\mbox{$\frac{\,{d+2p_{2}+2}\,}{4}$}+\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},\mbox{$\frac{\,{d+2p_{2}+4}\,}{4}$}+\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2}\\
1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\middle|r_{2}^{2}\right]+\text{c.c.};

(200)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\text{L},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{4\pi}r_{1}^{d/2+1+p_{1}}r_{2}^{d/2+1+p_{2}}\sum_{n_{1},n_{2}=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{n_{12}}}{n_{1}!n_{2}!}\Big[\text{e}^{\mathrm{i}\pi\overline{p}_{12}/2}+\text{e}^{\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-\mathrm{i}\pi(p_{12}+d)/2}\Big]\Big(\frac{r_{1}}{2}\Big)^{2n_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Big(\frac{r_{2}}{2}\Big)^{2n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Big[2n_{1}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+1+p_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,2n_{2}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+1+p_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,-n_{1}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,-n_{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\Big]+\text{c.c.}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{2\pi}\Big[\cos\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi\overline{p}_{12}}\,}{2}$}+\cos\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi(2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{12}+d)}\,}{2}$}\Big]r_{1}^{d/2+1+p_{1}}r_{2}^{d/2+1+p_{2}}\Big(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\Big)^{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle\Gamma\Big[\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+1+p_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+1+p_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\Big]

\displaystyle\times{}_{2}F_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}\mbox{$\frac{\,{d+2p_{1}+2}\,}{4}$}+\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},\mbox{$\frac{\,{d+2p_{1}+4}\,}{4}$}+\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2}\\
1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\middle|r_{1}^{2}\right]{}_{2}F_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}\mbox{$\frac{\,{d+2p_{2}+2}\,}{4}$}-\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},\mbox{$\frac{\,{d+2p_{2}+4}\,}{4}$}-\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2}\\
1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\middle|r_{2}^{2}\right]+\text{c.c.};

(201)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\text{BG},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle~\frac{-1}{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\sin\big[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(p_{12}+d)\big]r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+2}\sum_{n_{1},n_{2}=0}^{\infty}\bigg\{\frac{(-1)^{n_{12}}}{n_{1}!n_{2}!}\Big(\frac{r_{1}}{2}\Big)^{2n_{1}+2n_{2}}(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)_{-n_{1}}(\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)_{-n_{2}}

\displaystyle\times{}_{2}\mathcal{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}2n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+1,2n_{12}+p_{12}+d+2\\
2n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+2\end{matrix}\middle|-\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\right]\bigg\}+\text{c.c.}.

(202)

The nonlocal and local signal pieces, namely
\mathcal{I}_{\text{NL},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
and
\mathcal{I}_{\text{L},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
, can be written in more compact forms, as shown in (
29
) and (
30
). On the other hand, the result for the background piece
\mathcal{I}_{\text{BG},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
in (
D
) has resummed all
r_{1}/r_{2}
dependence into a hypergeometric function, which is typically what we would get from a partial Mellin-Barnes representation
[
72
]
. On the other hand, the result shown in (
31
) was expressed as a double series in
r_{1}
and
r_{1}/r_{2}
, which is typically what we would find from recursively solving the bootstrap equation
[
58
,
72
]
. In
[
72
]
, it was checked numerically that the partially resummed result (
D
) agrees with the bootstrapped double series (
31
). We shall prove analytically the equivalence of these two results in the next appendix.

Appendix E
From Partial Mellin-Barnes to Bootstrapped Series

As commented at the end of the previous appendix, the background piece of the tree seed integral can be computed in two different ways, namely the partial Mellin-Barnes representation and solving the bootstrap equations. They yield two results of different looks, given respectively by (
D
) and (
31
). In this appendix, we shall show the equivalence between the two by deriving (
31
) from (
D
).

First, we use the definition of the (dressed) hypergeometric function (
104
) and (
106
) to rewrite
{}_{2}\mathcal{F}_{1}
in (
D
) as a series:

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\text{BG},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle~\frac{-1}{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\sin\big[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(p_{12}+d)\big]r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+2}\sum_{n_{1},n_{2},\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{n_{12}+\ell}}{n_{1}!n_{2}!\ell!}\Big(\frac{r_{1}}{2}\Big)^{2n_{12}}\Big(\displaystyle\frac{\,{r_{1}}\,}{r_{2}}\Big)^{\ell}(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)_{-n_{1}}(\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)_{-n_{2}}

\displaystyle\times\displaystyle\frac{\,{\Gamma(\ell+2n_{12}+p_{12}+d+2)}\,}{\ell+2n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+1}+\text{c.c.}.

(203)

Using the new summation variable
m=n_{1}+n_{2}
, we can rewrite the summation as

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\text{BG},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle~\frac{-1}{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\sin\big[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(p_{12}+d)\big]r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+2}\sum_{m,\ell=0}^{\infty}\sum_{n_{2}=0}^{m}\binom{m}{n_{2}}\frac{(-1)^{m+\ell}}{m!\ell!}\Big(\frac{r_{1}}{2}\Big)^{2m}\Big(\displaystyle\frac{\,{r_{1}}\,}{r_{2}}\Big)^{\ell}(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)_{n_{2}-m}(\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)_{-n_{2}}

\displaystyle\times\displaystyle\frac{\,{\Gamma(\ell+2m+p_{12}+d+2)}\,}{\ell+2n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+1}

\displaystyle~+\frac{1}{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\sin\big[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(p_{12}+d)\big]r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+2}\sum_{m,\ell=0}^{\infty}\sum_{n_{2}=0}^{m}\binom{m}{n_{2}}\frac{(-1)^{m+\ell}}{m!\ell!}\Big(\frac{r_{1}}{2}\Big)^{2m}\Big(\displaystyle\frac{\,{r_{1}}\,}{r_{2}}\Big)^{\ell}(+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)_{n_{2}-m}(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)_{-n_{2}}

\displaystyle\times\displaystyle\frac{\,{\Gamma(\ell+2m+p_{12}+d+2)}\,}{\ell+2n_{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+1}

(204)

There are two summations in this expression. For the second summation, we change the summation variable
n_{2}\rightarrow m-n_{2}
:

\displaystyle\sum_{m,\ell=0}^{\infty}\sum_{n_{2}=0}^{m}\binom{m}{n_{2}}\frac{(-1)^{m+\ell}}{m!\ell!}\Big(\frac{r_{1}}{2}\Big)^{2m}\Big(\displaystyle\frac{\,{r_{1}}\,}{r_{2}}\Big)^{\ell}(+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)_{n_{2}-m}(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)_{-n_{2}}\displaystyle\frac{\,{\Gamma(\ell+2m+p_{12}+d+2)}\,}{\ell+2n_{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m,\ell=0}^{\infty}\sum_{n_{2}=0}^{m}\binom{m}{n_{2}}\frac{(-1)^{m+\ell}}{m!\ell!}\Big(\frac{r_{1}}{2}\Big)^{2m}\Big(\displaystyle\frac{\,{r_{1}}\,}{r_{2}}\Big)^{\ell}(+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)_{-n_{2}}(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)_{n_{2}-m}\displaystyle\frac{\,{\Gamma(\ell+2m+p_{12}+d+2)}\,}{\ell+2(m-n_{2})+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+1}.

(205)

Then, combining this result with the first summation in (
E
), we get:

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\text{BG},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle~\frac{-1}{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\sin\big[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(p_{12}+d)\big]r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+2}\sum_{m,\ell=0}^{\infty}\sum_{n_{2}=0}^{m}\binom{m}{n_{2}}\frac{(-1)^{m+\ell}}{m!\ell!}\Big(\frac{r_{1}}{2}\Big)^{2m}\Big(\displaystyle\frac{\,{r_{1}}\,}{r_{2}}\Big)^{\ell}(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)_{n_{2}-m}(\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)_{-n_{2}}

\displaystyle\times\displaystyle\frac{\,{2(m-2n_{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma(\ell+2m+p_{12}+d+2)}\,}{(\ell+2n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+1)[\ell+2(m-n_{2})+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+1]}.

(206)

Next, we finish the summation over
n_{2}
. Isolating the terms dependent on
n_{2}
, we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{n_{2}=0}^{m}\binom{m}{n_{2}}\displaystyle\frac{\,{(n_{2}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{m+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$})(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)_{n_{2}-m}(\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)_{-n_{2}}}\,}{(n_{2}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\ell-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2+p_{2}+1}\,}{2}$})(n_{2}-m-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\ell+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2+p_{2}+1}\,}{2}$})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{n_{2}=0}^{m}\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{n_{2}!}\displaystyle\frac{\,{\Gamma(-m+n_{2})}\,}{\Gamma(-m)}\displaystyle\frac{\,{(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)(n_{2}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{m+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$})}\,}{(n_{2}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\ell-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2+p_{2}+1}\,}{2}$})(n_{2}-m-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\ell+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2+p_{2}+1}\,}{2}$})}\Gamma\begin{bmatrix}-m+n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
1+n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{\Gamma(-m)}{}_{5}\mathcal{F}_{4}\left[\begin{matrix}-m,-\mbox{$\frac{\,{m+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}+1,\mbox{$\frac{\,{\ell-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2+p_{2}+1}\,}{2}$},-m-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\ell+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2+p_{2}+1}\,}{2}$},-m-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
-\mbox{$\frac{\,{m+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{\ell-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2+p_{2}+3}\,}{2}$},-m-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\ell+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2+p_{2}-1}\,}{2}$},1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\middle|1\right].

(207)

The dressed hypergeometric function
{}_{5}\mathcal{F}_{4}
in the last line can be simplified by using the Rogers–Dougall very well-poised sum for
{}_{5}\mathrm{F}_{4}

[
93
,
94
]
:

\displaystyle{}_{5}F_{4}\left[\begin{matrix}a,\tfrac{a}{2}+1,b,c,e\\
\tfrac{a}{2},a-b+1,a-c+1,a-e+1\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]

\displaystyle=\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}a-b+1,a-c+1,a-e+1,a-b-c-e+1\\
a+1,a-b-c+1,a-b-e+1,a-c-e+1\end{matrix}\right],

(208)

when
\text{Re}(b+c+d-a)<1
, or when the series terminates with
e=-n
(
n=0,1,2,\cdots
). Here we can choose

\displaystyle a=-m-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,

\displaystyle b=\frac{\ell-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}+\frac{d}{4},

\displaystyle c=-m-\frac{\ell+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}-\frac{d}{4},

\displaystyle e=-m.

(209)

Then, we get

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\text{BG},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}\sum_{m,\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{\left(-1\right)^{m+\ell}\sin[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(p_{12}+d)]}{\ell!}\Big(\frac{r_{1}}{2}\Big)^{2m}\Big(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\Big)^{\ell}r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+2}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}\tfrac{\ell-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+p_{2}+1}{2}+\tfrac{d}{4},-m-\tfrac{\ell+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+p_{2}+1}{2}-\tfrac{d}{4},2m+\ell+d+p_{12}+2\\
-\tfrac{\ell+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+p_{2}-1}{2}-\tfrac{d}{4},m+\tfrac{\ell-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+p_{2}+3}{2}+\tfrac{d}{4}\end{matrix}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m,\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell+1}\sin[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{2}$}(p_{12}+d)](\ell+1)_{2m+d+p_{12}+1}}{2^{2m+1}\big(\tfrac{\ell-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+p_{2}+1}{2}+\tfrac{d}{4}\big)_{m+1}\big(\tfrac{\ell+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+p_{2}+1}{2}+\tfrac{d}{4}\big)_{m+1}}r_{1}^{2m+d+p_{12}+2}\Big(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\Big)^{\ell},

(210)

which is nothing but the double series (
31
).

Appendix F
Bootstrapping Loop Correlators in Minkowski Spacetime

In this section, we compute the 4-point correlation with 1-loop massive scalar exchange in the
s
-channel in Minkowski spacetime. The diagram is again given by the left-hand side of Fig.
1
. It turns out a direct computation of SK time integrals is already complicated enough in
(d+1)
-dimensional Minkowski space. Therefore, we also take the spectral decomposition approach here. Below, we first compute the corresponding tree-level correlator, namely the right-hand side of Fig.
1
, and then derive a spectral function in flat space. Finally, we compute the 1-loop correlator by finishing the spectral integral over the tree-level correlator.

F.1
Tree-level correlator

We again apply the SK diagrammatic method
[
25
]
to compute the correlator. In flat space, algebra is much simplified. For instance, one of the homogeneous propagators for a scalar of mass
m
is given by

D_{m,>}(\mathbf{k};t_{1},t_{2})=\displaystyle\frac{\,{e^{-\mathrm{i}\sqrt{\mathbf{k}^{2}+m^{2}}(t_{1}-t_{2})}}\,}{2\sqrt{\mathbf{k}^{2}+m^{2}}},

(211)

and
D_{m,<}
is the complex conjugation of
D_{m,>}
. The four SK propagators
D_{\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}}
can be built as in (
3
) and (
4
).
Then, the
s
-channel diagram of the 4-point function can be computed as

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{\text{Mink}}_{m,\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}}=

\displaystyle-\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}\int_{-\infty}^{0}{\mathrm{d}}t_{1}{\mathrm{d}}t_{2}\,G_{\mathsf{a}}(\mathbf{k}_{1};\tau_{1})G_{\mathsf{a}}(\mathbf{k}_{2};\tau_{1})G_{\mathsf{b}}(\mathbf{k}_{3};\tau_{2})G_{\mathsf{b}}(\mathbf{k}_{4};\tau_{2})D_{m,\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}}(\mathbf{k}_{s};\tau_{1},\tau_{2}).

(212)

Here
G_{\mathsf{a}}(\mathbf{k}_{i},\tau)
are boundary propagators for the four external legs, and
D_{m,\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}}
is the bulk propagator for the intermediate states. We take all these lines to be massive scalars. The simple algebra of Minkowski space allows us to introduce (different) masses to all these legs without any pain. So, we associate
m_{i}

(i=1,2,3,4)
to the four external legs and
m
to the intermediate state. Then, we have
E_{i}\equiv\sqrt{\mathbf{k}_{i}^{2}+m_{i}^{2}}
and
E_{s}\equiv\sqrt{\mathbf{k}_{s}^{2}+m^{2}}
. With these quantities, the time integrals can be done directly. First, the two same-sign integrals read:

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{\text{Mink}}_{m,\pm\pm}=

\displaystyle-\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{32E_{1}E_{2}E_{3}E_{4}E_{s}}\bigg[\int_{-\infty}^{0}{\mathrm{d}}t_{1}\int_{-\infty}^{t_{1}}{\mathrm{d}}t_{2}\,e^{\pm\mathrm{i}(E_{12}-E_{s})t_{1}\pm\mathrm{i}(E_{34}+E_{s})t_{2}}

\displaystyle+\int_{-\infty}^{0}{\mathrm{d}}t_{1}\int_{t_{1}}^{0}{\mathrm{d}}t_{2}\,e^{\pm\mathrm{i}(E_{12}+E_{s})t_{1}\pm\mathrm{i}(E_{34}-E_{s})t_{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{32E_{1}E_{2}E_{3}E_{4}E_{s}(E_{12}+E_{s})(E_{34}+E_{s})}\bigg(1+\displaystyle\frac{\,{2E_{s}}\,}{E_{1234}}\bigg).

(213)

Then, the two opposite-sign integrals read:

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{\text{Mink}}_{m,s,\pm\mp}=

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{32E_{1}E_{2}E_{3}E_{4}E_{s}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}{\mathrm{d}}t_{1}{\mathrm{d}}t_{2}\,e^{\pm\mathrm{i}(E_{12}+E_{s})t_{1}\mp\mathrm{i}(E_{34}+E_{s})t_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{32E_{1}E_{2}E_{3}E_{4}E_{s}(E_{12}+E_{s})(E_{34}+E_{s})}.

(214)

Summing up the four integrals, we get the final answer for the tree-level 4-point diagram with
s
-channel massive scalar exchange:

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{m}^{\text{Mink}}\equiv\sum_{\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b}=\pm}\mathcal{T}_{m,\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}}^{\text{Mink}}=\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{8E_{1}E_{2}E_{3}E_{4}E_{s}(E_{12}+E_{s})(E_{34}+E_{s})}\bigg(1+\displaystyle\frac{\,{E_{s}}\,}{E_{1234}}\bigg).

(215)

This will be the resource for us to bootstrap the 1-loop correlator.

F.2
Spectral function

Now we turn to the spectral decomposition in flat space. The goal is to find a spectral function
\rho^{\text{Mink}}_{m^{2}}(m^{\prime\,2})
in Minkowski spacetime such that

\Big[D_{m}(x,y)\Big]^{2}=\int_{0}^{\infty}{\mathrm{d}}m^{\prime}\displaystyle\frac{\,{m^{\prime}}\,}{\pi\mathrm{i}}\rho_{m^{2}}^{\text{Mink}}(m^{\prime\,2})D_{m^{\prime}}(x,y),

(216)

or, in spatial-momentum space,

\int\displaystyle\frac{\,{{\mathrm{d}}^{d}\mathbf{q}}\,}{(2\pi)^{d}}D_{m,\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}}\Big(|\mathbf{q}|;t_{1},t_{2}\Big)D_{m,\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}}\Big(|\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{k}|;t_{1},t_{2}\Big)=\int_{0}^{\infty}{\mathrm{d}}m^{\prime}\displaystyle\frac{\,{m^{\prime}}\,}{\pi\mathrm{i}}\rho_{m^{2}}^{\text{Mink}}(m^{\prime\,2})D_{m^{\prime},\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}}\Big(|\mathbf{k}|;t_{1},t_{2}\Big).

(217)

It turns out convenient to start from the Euclidean version. Let us consider the product of a pair of scalar propagators
[D_{m}^{\text{Euc}}(x,y)]^{2}
in Euclidean space, and take the Fourier transform:

\Big[D_{m}^{\text{Eucl}}(x,y)\Big]^{2}=\int\displaystyle\frac{\,{{\mathrm{d}}^{d+1}k}\,}{(2\pi)^{d+1}}\,B_{m^{2}}^{\text{Eucl}}(k^{2})\text{e}^{\mathrm{i}k_{\mu}(x_{1}-x_{2})^{\mu}},

(218)

where
B_{m^{2}}^{\text{Eucl}}(k^{2})
is the 1-loop bubble function in Euclidean space. By the symmetry of the theory, the bubble function
B_{m^{2}}^{\text{Eucl}}(k^{2})
depends only on the magnitude of the (Euclidean) momentum
k^{2}=k_{\mu}k^{\mu}
.

Now, suppose that we know the bubble function
B_{m^{2}}^{\text{Eucl}}(k^{2})
. Then, it is straightforward to express the spectral function
\rho^{\text{Mink}}_{m^{2}}(m^{\prime\,2})
in terms of the bubble function. More explicitly, we have:

\displaystyle\int\frac{{\mathrm{d}}^{d+1}k}{(2\pi)^{d+1}}B_{m^{2}}^{\text{Eucl}}(k^{2})\text{e}^{\mathrm{i}k_{\mu}(x-y)^{\mu}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{2^{d}\pi^{d/2+1}\Gamma(\frac{d}{2})}\int_{0}^{\infty}{\mathrm{d}}k\,k^{d}\int_{0}^{\pi}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,\sin^{d-1}\theta\,\text{e}^{\mathrm{i}kr\cos\theta}B_{m^{2}}^{\text{Eucl}}(k^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}k}{(2\pi)^{(d+1)/2}}\frac{k^{(d+1)/2}}{r^{(d-1)/2}}\mathrm{J}_{(d-1)/2}(kr)B_{m^{2}}^{\text{Eucl}}(k^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}m^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{(d+1)/2}}\frac{\mathrm{i}}{\pi}\frac{m^{\prime\,(d+1)/2}}{r^{(d-1)/2}}\mathrm{K}_{(d-1)/2}(m^{\prime}r)\Big[B_{m^{2}}^{\text{Eucl}}(m^{\prime\,2}\text{e}^{\mathrm{i}\pi})-B_{m^{2}}^{\text{Eucl}}(m^{\prime\,2}\text{e}^{-\mathrm{i}\pi})\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}{\mathrm{d}}m^{\prime}\,\frac{m^{\prime}}{\pi\mathrm{i}}D_{m^{\prime}}^{\text{Eucl}}(x,y)\Big[B_{m^{2}}^{\text{Eucl}}(m^{\prime\,2}\text{e}^{-\mathrm{i}\pi})-B_{m^{2}}^{\text{Eucl}}(m^{\prime\,2}\text{e}^{\mathrm{i}\pi})\Big],

(219)

where
\mathrm{J}_{\nu}(z)
is the Bessel function and
\mathrm{K}_{\nu}(z)
is a modified Bessel function. Here we have used the fact that the Euclidean propagator for a scalar of mass
m
is given by

\displaystyle D_{m}^{\text{Eucl}}(x,y)=\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{(2\pi)^{(d+1)/2}}\Big(\displaystyle\frac{\,{m}\,}{r}\Big)^{(d-1)/2}\mathrm{K}_{(d-1)/2}(mr).

(220)

Now we can Wick-rotate back to the Minkowski spacetime. Then, comparing (
218
), (
F.2
), and (
216
), we find

\rho_{m^{2}}^{\text{Mink}}(m^{\prime\,2})=B_{m^{2}}^{\text{Eucl}}(m^{\prime\,2}\text{e}^{-\mathrm{i}\pi})-B_{m^{2}}^{\text{Eucl}}(m^{\prime\,2}\text{e}^{\mathrm{i}\pi}).

(221)

It remains to find the bubble function
B_{m^{2}}^{\text{Eucl}}(k^{2})
. To this end, we Fourier transform each of the two propagators
D_{m}^{\text{Eucl}}(x,y)
separately:

\displaystyle\Big[D_{m}^{\text{Eucl}}(x,y)\Big]^{2}=

\displaystyle\int\displaystyle\frac{\,{{\mathrm{d}}q_{1}^{d+1}}\,}{(2\pi)^{d+1}}\displaystyle\frac{\,{{\mathrm{d}}q_{2}^{d+1}}\,}{(2\pi)^{d+1}}\displaystyle\frac{\,{e^{\mathrm{i}(q_{1}+q_{2})_{\mu}(x-y)^{\mu}}}\,}{(q_{1}^{2}+m^{2})(q_{2}^{2}+m^{2})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\displaystyle\frac{\,{{\mathrm{d}}p^{d+1}}\,}{(2\pi)^{d+1}}\displaystyle\frac{\,{e^{\mathrm{i}p_{\mu}(x-y)^{\mu}}}\,}{[(p-q)^{2}+m^{2}](q^{2}+m^{2})},

(222)

where
p=q_{1}+q_{2}
and
q=q_{2}
. Comparing (
F.2
) with (
218
), we find:

\displaystyle B_{m^{2}}^{\text{Eucl}}(k^{2})=

\displaystyle\int\displaystyle\frac{\,{{\mathrm{d}}^{d+1}q}\,}{(2\pi)^{d+1}}\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{((k-q)^{2}+m^{2})(q^{2}+m^{2})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{\Gamma(\frac{3-d}{2})}\,}{(4\pi)^{(d+1)/2}}\int_{0}^{1}\displaystyle\frac{\,{{\mathrm{d}}\xi}\,}{[m^{2}+\xi(1-\xi)k^{2}]^{(3-d)/2}},

(223)

where we use the standard Feynman parametrization in the last step. Finally, using (
221
), we get an expression for the 1-loop spectral function in Minkowski spacetime:

\displaystyle\rho_{m^{2}}^{\text{Mink}}(m^{\prime\,2})=

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{\Gamma(\frac{3-d}{2})}\,}{(4\pi)^{(d+1)/2}}\int_{0}^{1}{\mathrm{d}}\xi\bigg\{\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{[m^{2}+\xi(1-\xi)m^{\prime\,2}e^{-\mathrm{i}\pi}]^{(3-d)/2}}-\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{[m^{2}+\xi(1-\xi)m^{\prime\,2}e^{\mathrm{i}\pi}]^{(3-d)/2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{\Gamma(\frac{3-d}{2})}\,}{(4\pi)^{(d+1)/2}}\int_{0}^{1}{\mathrm{d}}\xi\,2\mathrm{i}\,\text{Im}\,\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{[m^{2}-\xi(1-\xi)m^{\prime\,2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon]^{(3-d)/2}}.

(224)

F.3
1-loop correlator

Now we are ready to bootstrap the 1-loop correlator. Using the Feynman rule in SK formalism, the 1-loop correlator has the following form:

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}^{\text{Mink}}_{m}\equiv\sum_{\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b}=\pm}\mathcal{L}^{\text{Mink}}_{m,\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}}=

\displaystyle-\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}\sum_{\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b}=\pm}\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}\int_{-\infty}^{0}{\mathrm{d}}t_{1}{\mathrm{d}}t_{2}\,G_{\mathsf{a}}(\mathbf{k}_{1};\tau_{1})G_{\mathsf{a}}(\mathbf{k}_{2};\tau_{1})G_{\mathsf{b}}(\mathbf{k}_{3};\tau_{2})G_{\mathsf{b}}(\mathbf{k}_{4};\tau_{2})

\displaystyle\times\int\displaystyle\frac{\,{{\mathrm{d}}^{d}\mathbf{q}}\,}{(2\pi)^{d}}D_{m,\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}}\Big(|\mathbf{q}|;t_{1},t_{2}\Big)D_{m,\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}}\Big(|\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{k}_{s}|;t_{1},t_{2}\Big).

(225)

Comparing this expression with the tree amplitude (
212
) and using the spectral function (
216
), we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}^{\text{Mink}}_{m}=\int_{0}^{\infty}{\mathrm{d}}m^{\prime}\displaystyle\frac{\,{m^{\prime}}\,}{2\pi\mathrm{i}}\rho_{m^{2}}^{\text{Mink}}(m^{\prime\,2})\mathcal{T}^{\text{Mink}}_{m^{\prime}}.

(226)

Now, with the explicit expressions for the tree-level correlator
\mathcal{T}^{\text{Mink}}_{m}
in (
215
) and the spectral function
\rho_{m^{2}}^{\text{Mink}}(m^{\prime\,2})
in (
F.2
), we can compute the 1-loop correlator as

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{m}^{\text{Mink}}=\int_{0}^{\infty}{\mathrm{d}}m^{\prime}\displaystyle\frac{\,{m^{\prime}}\,}{2\pi\mathrm{i}}\displaystyle\frac{\,{(1+E_{s}/E_{1234})}\,}{8E_{1}E_{2}E_{3}E_{4}E_{s}(E_{12}+E_{s})(E_{34}+E_{s})}\rho_{m^{2}}^{\text{Mink}}(m^{\prime\,2}).

(227)

Change the integration variable from
m^{\prime}
to
E_{s}=\sqrt{\mathbf{k}_{s}^{2}+m^{\prime\,2}}
, we have
{\mathrm{d}}m^{\prime}(m^{\prime}/E_{s})={\mathrm{d}}E_{s}
. Then,

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{m}^{\text{Mink}}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}{\mathrm{d}}\xi\int_{E_{\text{min}}}^{\infty}\displaystyle\frac{\,{{\mathrm{d}}E_{s}}\,}{\pi}\displaystyle\frac{\,{(1+E_{s}/E_{1234})}\,}{8E_{1}E_{2}E_{3}E_{4}(E_{12}+E_{s})(E_{34}+E_{s})}

\displaystyle~\times\displaystyle\frac{\,{\Gamma(\frac{3-d}{2})}\,}{(4\pi)^{(d+1)/2}}\displaystyle\frac{\,{-\cos(\pi d/2)}\,}{[\xi(1-\xi)(E_{s}^{2}-E_{\text{min}}^{2})]^{(3-d)/2}}.

(228)

Here
E_{\text{min}}\equiv\sqrt{\mathbf{k}_{s}^{2}+m^{2}/[\xi(1-\xi)]}
. The
E_{s}
integral can be finished directly. In the
d\rightarrow 3
limit, the result is

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{m}^{\text{Mink}}=

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{256\pi^{2}E_{1}E_{2}E_{3}E_{4}E_{1234}}\bigg[\displaystyle\frac{\,{2}\,}{3-d}-\gamma_{E}+\log 4\pi+2

\displaystyle~+\displaystyle\frac{\,{2}\,}{E_{12}-E_{34}}\int_{0}^{1}{\mathrm{d}}\xi\,\bigg(E_{34}\log\displaystyle\frac{\,{E_{12}+E_{\text{min}}}\,}{\mu_{R}}-E_{12}\log\displaystyle\frac{\,{E_{34}+E_{\text{min}}}\,}{\mu_{R}}\bigg)\bigg].

(229)

This is the final result for the 1-loop correlator in Minkowski spacetime. We can now directly use
\overline{\text{MS}}
scheme to subtract the divergent piece
\propto 2/(3-d)-\gamma_{E}+\log 4\pi
. It is also of interest to have an expression for the renormalized loop correlator in the large mass limit
m\rightarrow\infty
:

\displaystyle\Big[\mathcal{L}_{m}^{\text{Mink}}\Big]_{\overline{\text{MS}}}=

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{256\pi^{2}E_{1}E_{2}E_{3}E_{4}E_{1234}}\bigg[\log\displaystyle\frac{\,{\mu_{R}^{2}}\,}{m^{2}}-\displaystyle\frac{\,{E_{12}E_{34}+\mathbf{k}_{s}^{2}}\,}{6m^{2}}+\mathcal{O}\Big(\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{m^{3}}\Big)\bigg].

(230)
